# Workstream 7 - Systematic coastline coverage: every segment of the Cornish and Scilly shoreline

Built 2026-11-21 16:56 UTC by `fishing/tools/coastline/01_build_segments.py`, `02_enrich.py`, `03_score.py`. Full table (all 138 columns, including the 36-direction fetch) in `coastline/segments_scored.csv`.

## 1. How the shoreline was divided

- **Base line:** Ordnance Survey OS OpenMap Local (Open Government Licence), `TidalBoundary` *High Water Mark* lines, tiles SW, SX, SV, SS (downloaded 2026-11-21 ~15:18 UTC from api.os.uk). The high-water line runs up every estuary and creek to the Normal Tidal Limit. The *Low Water Mark* lines give the low-water shore position and intertidal width; `Foreshore` polygons give drying ground.
- **County membership:** ONS Counties and Unitary Authorities (December 2024) full-extent (BFE) polygons for Cornwall and the Isles of Scilly; Devon and Plymouth shores are excluded (Tamar east bank, Plymouth Sound east, Eddystone - administratively Plymouth). Offshore rocks outside every polygon were assigned to the nearest authority (list in the tool output `offshore_pieces_assignment.json`).
- **Consecutive order:** the mainland high-water line was chained into one continuous line from **Marsland Mouth (Devon border, north coast) clockwise round Land's End to the tidal limit of the Tamar**; small breaks in the OS line work (median 3 m; stream mouths, bridges, structures) were bridged with straight connectors. It was then cut into equal pieces of **no more than 1,000 m** (IDs `M0001`-`M0971`). Side pieces, islands and rocks are listed after the mainland in order of the adjacent mainland chainage; Scilly follows; then detached drying reefs and banks (land that meets water only below high water).
- **Totals: 2009 segments, 1243 km of shoreline; longest segment 999 m.**

| Group | Segments | Shoreline km | Separate pieces | Longest segment m |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Detached drying reefs, ledges and banks (land only below high water) - Cornwall | 228 | 61.6 | 211 | 970 |
| Detached drying reefs and ledges - Isles of Scilly | 130 | 31.8 | 128 | 946 |
| Mainland side pieces (harbour interiors, branches, river islands - open lines) | 102 | 16.7 | 96 | 893 |
| Cornish islands, islets and rocks standing above high water (closed rings) | 300 | 49.6 | 295 | 878 |
| Mainland backbone (Marsland Mouth to the Tamar tidal limit, consecutive) | 971 | 970.2 | 1 | 999 |
| Isles of Scilly (all islands and rocks above high water) | 278 | 113.1 | 207 | 995 |

## 2. What was computed for every segment (first principles) and from what

| Descriptor | Method | Source | Quality |
|---|---|---|---|
| Aspect | seaward normal of the segment (local) and fetch-weighted open-water direction (`aspect_open`) | geometry | good |
| Wave fetch, 36 directions | ray casting from a point 15 m seaward to the first land (OS high-water lines within the tiles; Natural Earth 1:10m land beyond: Ireland, Wales, France), capped at 500 km | OS + Natural Earth | good; no refraction/diffraction |
| Setting | open coast (>= 6 directions with fetch > 10 km) / semi-enclosed bay or estuary mouth / estuary-ria-harbour / tidal river-creek (max fetch < 1.5 km) | derived | good |
| Intertidal width | distance from the segment midpoint to the OS low-water line | OS | good (OS low water = mean low water, not LAT) |
| Depth close in | EMODnet Digital Bathymetry (DTM, 1/16 arc-minute = ~115 m x 74 m cells, depths below LAT), max depth within 150 / 300 / 500 / 1000 / 2000 m of the low-water shore point; distance to the 2 / 5 / 10 / 20 / 30 / 50 m depth | EMODnet Bathymetry WCS `emodnet:mean`, downloaded 2026-11-21 15:20 UTC | **coarse**: gullies, ledges and drop-offs narrower than ~100 m are invisible; many estuaries have no cells |
| Seabed type | point-in-polygon at 100 / 300 / 1000 m seaward of low water, plus nearest mapped polygon: EMODnet Geology seabed substrate (1:50k multiscale, 1:250k, 1:1M Folk classes) and EUSeaMap 2025 (substrate, biozone, energy) | EMODnet Geology WFS, EMODnet Seabed Habitats WFS, downloaded 15:20-15:25 UTC | **thin inshore**: broad-scale modelled map; no mapped seabed within 300 m for most segments (see section 5) |
| Kelp | not mapped anywhere at this scale. Proxy only: rock substrate in the infralittoral biozone (kelp zone) | EUSeaMap | THIN (inference) |
| Bedrock | BGS Geology 625k bedrock polygon just landward of the segment | BGS OGC API, downloaded 15:20 UTC | good at 1:625k |
| Outflows | OS OpenMap Local stream line ends within 25 m and river polygons within 10 m of the segment | OS | good for mapped streams; outfalls/pipes not included |
| Names | three nearest coastal features within 1.5 km and nearest settlement | OS Open Names | good |
| Tidal flow | NOT computed per segment. Only proxies: headland index (number of directions with > 10 km fetch) and the coarse ocean-model currents at the reference points (`sea_state.md` section 6); literature in `tidal_streams.md` | - | **THIN for every segment** |
| Reefs / gullies / sandbanks | detached drying reefs and banks are themselves segments (groups DRY); mapped rock within 500 m is recorded; gullies are below the resolution of every dataset used | OS Foreshore, EUSeaMap | THIN for gullies |
| Charts | Admiralty / Navionics charts could not be accessed programmatically - NOT USED | - | gap |
| Man-made structure (piers, harbour walls) | OpenStreetMap could not be reached (Overpass servers refused/timed out); structures appear only through the documented-marks overlay | - | gap |
| Conditions on 22 Nov | wind and offshore waves: multi-model median at the nearest of 39 reference points (`weather.md`, `sea_state.md`); nearshore wave height = offshore Hs x sqrt(directional energy fraction reaching the segment through its open fetch directions, cos^4 or cos^8 spreading) plus fetch-limited local wind sea where the upwind fetch is < 25 km; tide phase from the nearest EasyTide port | this study | indicative only |
| Documentary overlay | merged known marks within 600 m of the segment midpoint (`marks.md`) | workstream 6 | - |

## 3. The provisional score

**H (habitat potential, 0-100)** = the best of five habitat guilds, each 0-1, times a provisional species-value weight:

| Guild | Meaning | Formula (rockness, sandness in [0,1]; d150/d300 = max depth within 150/300 m; open = 1 open coast, 0.6 semi-enclosed, 0 estuary) | Value weight |
|---|---|---|---|
| SURF | surf / open sandy beach (bass, rays, turbot, flounder, whiting) | sandness x beachness x clip(1 - d300/15, 0.2, 1) x open x (0.85 + 0.075 x outflows[max 2]); beachness = clip(intertidal width/150, 0.3, 1) where a beach is named or sediment is mapped at 100 m with >= 60 m of foreshore, else 0.25 | 1.0 |
| EDGE | reef-meets-sand mixed ground (bass, huss, rays, pollack) | edge x (0.3 + 0.7 x clip(d300/12)) x open x (0.85 + 0.075 x outflows); edge = 1.0 rock and sediment both mapped within 300 m, 0.7 rock within 300 m and sediment within 1 km, 0.6 sediment within 300 m and rock within 500 m-1 km, 0.35 both only at distance | 1.0 |
| ROUGH | rough ground / deep-water rock (pollack, wrasse, conger, huss) | rockness x (0.15 + 0.85 x clip(d300/25)^0.7) x (0.7 + 0.3 x headland index) x (0.6 inside estuaries) | 0.9 |
| STEEP | steep-to beach with depth close in (whiting, rays, bass, codling) | sandness x clip(d150/8) x open | 0.8 |
| EST | estuary / creek (flounder, bass, mullet) | [estuary 1, semi-enclosed 0.5] x (0.45 + 0.25[channel >= 3 m within 300 m] + 0.15[flats 50-800 m] + 0.15[stream/river] + 0.10[>= 10 m]) | 0.7 |

rockness = 1.0 rock mapped at 100 or 300 m; 0.7 rock within 500 m or at 1000 m; 0.5 inferred (open coast, intertidal < 40 m, no beach name); else 0.1. sandness = 1.0 sediment mapped at 100/300 m and an OS-named beach within 500 m; 0.8 sediment mapped; 0.7 named beach only; 0.3 sediment only at >= 400-1000 m; else 0.

**P (provisional score for 22 Nov, 0-100)** = max over guilds of value x H_guild x best 3-hour mean of [wave suitability(nearshore Hs) x light factor x tide factor], with a 0.7 spate factor on tidal rivers/creeks (heavy rain forecast, rivers already above normal). Wave suitability: SURF ideal 0.8-1.6 m (0 below 0.2 m, 0.55 at 2.5 m, 0.2 at 3.5 m); EDGE ideal 0.6-1.8 m (0.45 at 2.8 m); ROUGH ideal <= 1.2 m (0.55 at 2.2 m, 0.15 at 3.5 m); STEEP ideal 0.5-1.8 m; EST ideal < 0.6 m; SURF and STEEP x 0.85 under an onshore wind > 25 kn. Light: darkness and dusk/dawn 1.0, daylight 0.6 (EST 0.9). Tide: flood from LW+1 1.0; first hour of flood 0.85; HW to HW+1 0.9; to HW+3 0.75; later ebb 0.65 (EST: flood 1.0, early ebb 0.8, later ebb 0.5).

**These weights are provisional and deliberately simple.** They exist so that every segment has a comparable, reproducible number in Phase 1; Phase 2 must re-derive the weights from `species_sst.md`, add water clarity, and re-score. The score knows nothing about fish records - the documentary evidence is the separate overlay column.

Distribution of P over all 2009 segments: median 30, 75th pct 42, 90th 54, 95th 60, 99th 76, max 91. Segments with P >= the 90th percentile are carried into Phase 2 as candidates whether or not anyone has documented fishing there.

| Best guild on the day | Segments | Mean P | Max P |
|---|---|---|---|
| EDGE - reef-meets-sand mixed ground (bass, huss, rays, pollack) | 387 | 40 | 91 |
| EST - estuary / creek (flounder, bass, mullet) | 785 | 30 | 70 |
| ROUGH - rough ground / deep-water rock (pollack, wrasse, conger, huss) | 704 | 31 | 86 |
| STEEP - steep-to beach with depth close in (whiting, rays, bass, codling) | 72 | 37 | 76 |
| SURF - surf / open sandy beach (bass, rays, turbot, flounder, whiting) | 61 | 31 | 84 |
